- **sprint-roll**: Roll the current sprint into the next and rebalance every sprint after it to one measured cap. Moves what did not finish, pools every open ticket across the window and places it forward by dependency and criterion coverage, sends the remainder to Backlog, applies the beta-critical rule to the next two sprints, and maps every ticket no carrier list names to a release criterion from its own Gate line.
